Let's design `project_sum_bounds(y, target)`:
Use binary search. But for small n, we can implement robust:
```
def project_sum_bounds(y, target):
    n=len(y)
    l=min(y)-1.0
    hi=max(y)+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       t=(l+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(y-t,0,1)
       ss=s.sum()
       if ss > target: l=t
       else: hi=t
    x=np.clip(y-(l+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(x<1)[0]
       else: idx=np.where(x>0)[0]
       if len(idx)>0:
          # distribute evenly up to slack
          step=diff/len(idx)
          x[idx]=np.clip(x[idx]+step,0,1)
          # repeat few times
    return x
```
Better exact: After binary, residual small; we can adjust one interior with enough slack. Use loop 10.

For parent initial_h_values, ensure sum target exactly maybe by projection.

Gradient active:
```
def active_gradient(h, q, beta=20, max_lags=40):
    n=len(h); m=n-1
    idx = np.argpartition(q, -max_lags)[-max_lags:]  # not sorted
    vals = q[idx]
    vals = vals - vals.max()
    weights = np.exp(beta*vals); weights/=weights.sum()
    # if beta large underflow all? subtract max ok; if all zero, uniform.
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for p,w in zip(idx,weights):
       if w < 1e-12: continue
       l = m - p
       # add gradient lag
       if l==0: g += w*(1-2*h)
       elif l>0: ...
    # normalize? Keep scale dx? Not necessary; line search adjusts.
    return g
```
Note q values scale ~n/4; beta*diff. For target differences small (~1e-4 n?), beta needs large? q scale ~40, objective difference 1e-6? q difference dx? C=2q/n -> q diff = C diff *n/2 ~0.00005*80=0.004. To distinguish top, beta ~100-1000. But underflow okay. We can use beta 50,100,200. For gradient of true max, any positive weights on active set; with beta moderate smooth. To target exact, perhaps use only top 5 uniform. Could try both smooth beta and hard top.

def pair_local_search(h, q, n, best_c, time_limit):
  h = h.copy(); q = make_q(h); c=c5(q,n)
  # precompute h_rev? but h changes.
  it=0
  while time ok:
    g=active_gradient(h,q,beta=50,max_lags=20)
    pos = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:P] # high g
    neg = np.argsort(g)[:P] # low g
    improved=False
    tried=set()
    for i in neg:
       for j in pos:
          if i==j: continue
          # orientation inc i (low), dec j (high)
          maxd = min(1-h[i], h[j])
          if maxd>1e-9: try_delta(i,j,maxd)
          # random orientation occasionally
    random pairs...
    if not improved break
```

def local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, time_limit, seed):
   h=h.copy(); q=make_q(h); cur=cmax; best_local...
   dx=2/n
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   no_improve=0
   while time ok and no_improve<3:
      g=active_gradient(h,q,beta=30,max_lags=16)
      # remove NaN
      if not finite break
      low=np.argsort(g)[:P]
      high=np.argsort(g)[::-1][:P]
      tried=set()
      improved=False
      # gradient-guided
      for i in low:
        for j in high:
          if i==j: continue
          key=(i,j)
          res=try_pair(... orient True) # inc low, dec high
          if res: improved=True; ...
          # also reverse if slack
      # random pairs
      for _ in range(rand_count):
         i,j = rng.choice(n,2,replace=False)
         try both orients
      if not improved:
         no_improve+=1
      else: no_improve=0
      # recompute q every sweep
      q=make_q(h); cur=...
      if cur<best_c: best_c=...
   return h,q,best_c
```

For full h optimization:
```
def optimize_full(h, n, budget):
   h=project(h,n/2)
   q=make_q(h); cur=c5(q); best=cur
   # projected gradient
   for phase in range(...):
      for beta in [20,50,100,200]:
         for it in range(200):
            g=active_gradient...
            if norm <1e-10 break
            dir=-g/norm
            # momentum? simple
            accepted=False
            for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]*lr_base:
                h_try=project(h + factor*dir, target)
                c=compute
                if c<best - 1e-15:
                   accept; accepted=True; break
            if not accepted: break
      # maybe no improvement
   # pair local search
   h,q,best = local_pair_search(...)

def smooth_obj_grad(x, beta, weight_top=0):
   q=make_q(x)
   qmax=np.max(q)
   # use all q or top? Use all with shift to avoid overflow.
   shifted=q-qmax
   # If beta large, sum exp. For n=160, q range length maybe 100; okay.
   exps=np.exp(beta*shifted)
   s=exps.sum()
   obj = (qmax + np.log(s)/beta) * dx
   if grad:
      w=exps/s
      # compute gradient over all lags with weights. But all 319 lags; constructing gradient by loop O(319*n) ~50k, okay.
      g=np.zeros(n)
      for p,wp in enumerate(w): if wp>1e-10: l=m-p; add w*grad_lag
   return obj, g

```
def add_lag_gradient(g, h, l, w):
  n=len(h)
  if w==0: return
  if l==0: g += w*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
  elif l>0:
    m=n-l
    g[:m] += w*(1.0 - h[l:l+m])
    g[l:] -= w*h[:m]
  else:
    a=-l; m=n-a
    g[a:] += w*(1.0 - h[:m])
    g[:m] -= w*h[a:a+m]
```

             if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                h=h2; q=q2; c=...; best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); accepted=True; break
             elif c2 < c - 1e-14: # accept if improves current even if not global? To continue descent
                h=h2; q=q2; c=c2; if c<best_c: update; accepted=True; break
         if not accepted: break
      # optionally use raw max subgradient? active gradient already.
   # pair search
   h,q,best_c = local_pair_search(h,q,best_c, time, seed)
   # SLSQP polish if n<=200 and time
   ...
   return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

def fix_exact_sum(h, target):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
   diff=target - np.sum(h)
   for _ in range(10):
      if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
      if diff >0:
         mask=h<1.0
         if not mask.any(): break
         # pick largest slack one by one? add min(diff,1-h[idx])
         idx=np.where(mask)[0]
         order=np.argsort(1.0 - h[idx])[::-1]
         for idx2 in order:
            add=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx2])
            h[idx2]+=add; diff-=add
            if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      else: ...
   return h
```

def local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, time_limit, start_time, budget_s, seed):
    n=len(h); target=n/2; dx=2.0/n; cur=cmax
    h=h.copy(); q=make_q(h); cur=...
    fracs=(1.0,0.5,0.75,0.25,0.875,0.125,0.375)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_imp=0
    sweep=0
    while time check and no_imp<4:
       sweep+=1
       g=active_gradient(h,q,50,20)
       # filter nan
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
       P=min(16,n//2)
       low=np.argsort(g)[:P]
       high=np.argsort(g)[::-1][:P]
       improved=False
       # Guided pairs
       for i in low:
          for j in high:
             if i==j: continue
             # orientation inc i
             res=evaluate_pair_move(h,q,i,j,True,cur)
             if res: accept...
             if i!=j:
                res=evaluate_pair_move(h,q,i,j,False,cur)
             time check maybe every 100
       # Random pairs count depending time
       rand_count=300 if n<200 else 150
       for _ in range(rand_count):
          if time check: break
          i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n)); if same continue
          res=evaluate_pair_move(... choose random orientation)
       if improved: no_imp=0 else no_imp+=1
       if not improved:
          # maybe try global gradient step? Could call projected_gradient one more
          pass
       # Rebalance
       if sweep%3==0:
          h=project_simplex(h,target); q=make_q(h); cur=...
       if cur<best_c: best_c=cur
```

But copying for each frac; fractions 6. Fine.

SLSQP polish:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, time_limit):
  n=len(h)
  if n>256 or remaining_time()<60: return h,c
  _N=n; _TGT=n/2; _DX=2/n
  h0=project(h,_TGT)
  c0=c5(h0)
  for beta in [50,100,300]:
     _BETA=beta
     try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj, h0, method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0,1)]*n, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':con_sum}], options={'maxiter':80,'iprint':0,'ftol':1e-12})
       h_new=project(res.x,_TGT); c=c5(h_new)
       if c<c0: h0=h_new;c0=c
     except: pass
    return h0,c0
```
Defining `smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x):
   q=make_q(x); mx=q.max(); th=_BETA
   shifted=q-mx
   # If th*shifted too negative, exps sum maybe 1; if too positive? fine.
   exps=np.exp(th*shifted)
   s=exps.sum()
   if s<=0: s=1.0
   return (mx + np.log(s)/th)*_DX
```

```

```

Let's write project:
```
def project_simplex(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n=y.size
    # if already close and bounds, fix
    if np.all(y >= -1e-12) and np.all(y <= 1+1e-12) and abs(y.sum()-target)<1e-10:
       x=np.clip(y,0,1); return fix_sum(x,target)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
       t=0.5*(lo+hi)
       x=np.clip(y - t, 0.0, 1.0)
       s=float(np.sum(x))
       if s > target:
          lo=t
       else:
          hi=t
    x=np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    return fix_sum(x,target)
```
But if y values all clipped to 1 but target small? t range extends beyond. Good.
`fix_sum`:
```
def fix_sum(x,target):
   x=np.clip(x,0,1).copy()
   diff=target - float(np.sum(x))
   for _ in range(20):
      if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
      if diff > 0:
         idx=np.where(x < 1.0)[0]
         if idx.size==0: break
         # choose one with most slack
         idx=idx[np.argmax(1.0-x[idx])]
         add=min(diff, 1.0-x[idx])
         x[idx]+=add; diff-=add
      else:
         idx=np.where(x > 0.0)[0]
         if idx.size==0: break
         idx=idx[np.argmax(x[idx])]
         sub=min(-diff, x[idx])
         x[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
   return x
```
This may not distribute if diff large; but after binary diff tiny. For initial random y not close, binary handles.

But binary 50 iters plus fix_sum loops okay. Could optimize by using previous x as y? no.

def optimize_full(h, time_alloc, seed):
    global _N, _TGT, _DX
    start_local=time.time()
    n=len(h); _N=n; _TGT=n/2.0; _DX=2.0/n
    h=project_simplex(h,_TGT)
    q=make_q(h); c= float(np.max(q))*_DX
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    def local_ok? No nested. Use inline checks.
    # projected gradient
    beta_values = [0, 10, 40, 120]
    max_iter_beta = [120,80,50,30]
    for bi,beta in enumerate(beta_values):
       max_iter = max_iter_beta[bi]
       momentum=np.zeros(n)
       for it in range(max_iter):
           if (time.time()-start_local) > time_alloc*0.45 or remaining_time()<120: break? Need reserve. We'll just check time_alloc*0.4.
           g=active_gradient(h,q,beta,24)
           norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
           if norm <1e-12: break
           dirn = -g/norm
           momentum = 0.7*momentum + 0.3*dirn
           mn=np.linalg.norm(momentum)
           if mn==0: dirn_update=dirn
           else: dirn_update=momentum/mn
           # if momentum direction worse? use plain if no? simple use dirn_update.
           accepted=False
           lrs = [0.3,0.15,0.05,0.5,0.1,0.02,0.005,1.0]
           for lr in lrs:
                h2=project_simplex(h + lr*dirn_update, _TGT)
                q2=make_q(h2); c2=float(np.max(q2))*_DX
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                    h=h2; q=q2; c=c2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); accepted=True; break
                if c2 < c - 1e-14:
                    h=h2; q=q2; c=c2; if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); accepted=True; break
           if not accepted:
               # try plain dirn if momentum maybe caused fail? maybe one more
               # skip
               break
       # if no improvement? continue
    # Pair search
    pair_alloc = max(20.0, time_alloc*0.5)
    h,q,best_c = local_pair_search(h,q,best_c,start_local,time_alloc,pair_alloc,seed)
    # SLSQP
    if time_alloc > 80 and remaining_time()>80 and n <= 220:
       slsqp_alloc = max(20.0, min(120.0, time_alloc*0.3))
       h,best_c = slsqp_polish(h, slsqp_alloc)
       q=make_q(h)
       h,best_c = local_pair_search(h,q,best_c,start_local,time_alloc, min(pair_alloc, remaining_time?) , seed+1)
    return best_h,best_c,n

`local_pair_search` uses start_local/time_alloc. It should also check global remaining.
```
def local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, start_local, total_alloc, pair_alloc, seed):
   n=len(h); target=n/2; dx=2/n
   h=h.copy(); q=make_q(h); cur=float(np.max(q))*dx
   if cur<best_c: best_c=cur
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   noimp=0
   sweeps=0
   fracs=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.75,0.125,0.875,0.375)
   while sweeps < 12 and noimp<4 and (time.time()-start_local)<total_alloc and remaining_time()>5:
      sweeps+=1
      if noimp==0:
         g=active_gradient(h,q,30,20)
         P=min(14,n//3)
         low=np.argsort(g)[:P]
         high=np.argsort(g)[::-1][:P]
      else:
         g=None
      improved=False
      if g is not None:
         for ii in low:
            for jj in high:
               if ii==jj: continue
               # orientation inc low (ii), dec high (jj)
               move=pair_move_best(h,q,ii,jj,True,cur,fracs)
               if move is not None: accept...
               # reverse orientation maybe if slack
               move=pair_move_best(h,q,ii,jj,False,cur,fracs)
               if move...
               if time check...
      # random
      rnd_count=400 if n<=200 else 200
      for r in range(rnd_count):
         if time check: break
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
         if i==j: continue
         if rng.rand()<0.5:
             move=pair_move_best(h,q,i,j,True,cur,fracs)
         else:
             move=pair_move_best(h,q,i,j,False,cur,fracs)
         if move...
      if improved:
         noimp=0
         # recompute every 3 sweeps
         if sweeps %3==0:
            h=project_simplex(h,target); q=make_q(h); cur=cmax; if cur<best_c...
      else:
         noimp+=1
         # maybe if noimp==2, add tiny random noise and project to escape? optional
         if noimp==2:
             h2=project_simplex(h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n), target)
             q2=make_q(h2); c2=...
             if c2 < cur + 1e-6: h=h2; q=q2; cur=c2
   # final recompute
   h=project_simplex(h,target); q=make_q(h); cur=...
   if cur<best_c: best_c=cur
   return h,q,best_c
```
`pair_move_best` top-level:
```
def pair_move_best(h,q,i,j,inc_first,current_c,fracs):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    if inc_first:
       maxd = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
    else:
       maxd = min(1.0-h[j], h[i])
    if maxd <= 1e-12: return None
    best=None
    for frac in fracs:
       d=frac*maxd
       if d<=1e-10: continue
       h2=h.copy(); q2=q.copy()
       if inc_first:
          add_coord_update(q2,h2,i,d); h2[i]=h2[i]+d
          add_coord_update(q2,h2,j,-d); h2[j]=h2[j]-d
       else:
          add_coord_update(q2,h2,i,-d); h2[i]=h2[i]-d
          add_coord_update(q2,h2,j,d); h2[j]=h2[j]+d
       # if values within [0,1]
       if h2[i] <= 1+1e-12 and h2[i]>=-1e-12 and ...:
          c=float(np.max(q2))*dx
          if c < current_c - 1e-15:
             if best is None or c < best[2]: best=(h2,q2,c)
       # optional break if c < current_c - 1e-7? no
    return best
```
This tries all fracs; if best found, returns best. For speed, if a frac gives very close to theoretical? no.

`slsqp_polish` top-level:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, time_alloc, seed):
    n=len(h); start=time.time()
    global _N,_TGT,_DX,_BETA
    _N=n; _TGT=n/2.0; _DX=2.0/n
    h=project_simplex(h,_TGT); c=c5_of_h(h)
    if time_alloc<15 or n>220 or remaining_time()<40: return h,c
    beta_list=[30,80,200]
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.time()-start > time_alloc or remaining_time()<30: break
       _BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_obj, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':con_sum}], jac=smooth_jac, options={'maxiter':40,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
          h_new=project_simplex(res.x,_TGT)
          c_new=c5_of_h(h_new)
          if c_new < c -1e-15:
             h=h_new; c=c_new
       except Exception:
          pass
    h=project_simplex(h,_TGT); c=c5_of_h(h)
    return h,c
```
`con_sum(x)` uses `_TGT`. `smooth_obj` and `smooth_jac` top-level. They assume `_BETA` set. Need compute dx. In `smooth_jac`, use `_DX` to scale gradient. `bounds` list with n tuples; no lambda.

Potential issue: `minimize` may call `smooth_jac` after modifying x out of bounds? It should bound. If sum constraint causes, okay.

`smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x):
    q=make_q(x)
    mx=float(np.max(q))
    shifted=q-mx
    th=_BETA
    # if th huge, underflow
    exps=np.exp(th*shifted)
    s=float(np.sum(exps))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s): s=1.0
    val = mx + np.log(s)/th
    return float(val)*_DX
```
`smooth_jac` similar but also compute w. If beta <=0? We won't call. For numerical stability, if mx nan? no.
```
def smooth_jac(x):
   q=make_q(x); mx=...; exps...; w=exps/s
   m=len(x)-1; g=np.zeros(len(x))
   thresh=1e-10
   for p in range(len(w)):
       wp=w[p]*_DX
       if wp > thresh:
          l=m-p
          add_lag_gradient(g,x,l,wp)
   return g
```
If w sum 1 and dx ~0.01, wp maybe ~0.01, threshold 1e-10 fine.

`add_lag_gradient` modifies g in place. For l>0, `g[l:]` length m? If l=n-1, m=1, g[l:] length 1. Good.

def line_search_return(h, c, best_c, direction, target, dx):
    lrs=(0.2,0.05,0.5,0.1,0.02,0.005,0.002,0.8,0.001)
    for lr in lrs:
        h2=project_simplex(h + lr*direction, target)
        q2=make_q(h2)
        c2=float(np.max(q2))*dx
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
            return h2, q2, c2
        if c2 < c - 1e-14:
            return h2, q2, c2
    return None, None, None
```

def save_candidate(lst, h, n, c):
    if h is None:
       return
    h=np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
       return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       return
    h=project_simplex(h, n/2.0)
    c2=c5_of_h(h)

Need `interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(src_h, src_n, dst_n):
   src_h=np.asarray(src_h,float)
   if src_h.size != src_n: src_h=src_h[:src_n]
   if src_n == dst_n: return src_h.copy()
   xp=(np.arange(src_n)+0.5)*2.0/float(src_n)
   xq=(np.arange(dst_n)+0.5)*2.0/float(dst_n)
   return np.interp(xq, xp, src_h)
```

   if beta<=0: uniform else softmax

   best_h = fix_sum(best_h,target); final_c=c5_exact(best_h)
```
If fix worsens, unavoidable but small. Good.

Then evaluate_erdos_solution:
```
try:
    eval_c=evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
    if np.isfinite(eval_c) and eval_c < final_c + 1e-12:
        final_c=float(eval_c)
except Exception:
    pass
```
If eval_c slightly lower, okay. If much lower, we follow official. Could be dangerous if our h invalid? official likely exact. Use min. Return.

Need if best_h is None fallback. We ensure.

Let's now compose final code. Ensure imports. Use `math` for sqrt. No printing.

One final thought: The `c5_of_h` in `add_candidate` projects and evaluates. For binary random with n odd, h has 0.5 at middle, sum target? project. Good.

Potential issue: `np.linalg.lstsq` in lowdim may be slow for n=384,K=7? trivial. If ref_h length not n, pass None. lowdim for best_n only.

Potential issue: `minimize` Powell with objective that projects to simplex and c5 maybe non-smooth; xtol/gtol maybe not accepted if 0? okay.

Now final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_START_TIME = 0.0
_BUDGET_S = 1300.0

_POLY_N = 0
_POLY_BASIS = None
_POLY_TARGET = 0.0

_N = 0
_TGT = 0.0
_DX = 0.0
_BETA = 1.0


def remaining_time():
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET_S - (time.time() - _START_TIME) - 2.0)


def fix_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = float(target) - float(np.sum(x))
    for _ in range(30):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(x < 1.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            idx = idx[int(np.argmax(1.0 - x[idx]))]
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - x[idx])
            x[idx] = x[idx] + add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = np.where(x > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            idx = idx[int(np.argmax(x[idx]))]
            sub = min(-diff, x[idx])
            x[idx] = x[idx] - sub
            diff += sub
    return x


def project_simplex(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return y
    if np.all(np.isfinite(y)) and np.all(y >= -1e-12) and np.all(y <= 1.0 + 1e-12) and abs(float(np.sum(y)) - float(target)) < 1e-11:
        return fix_sum(np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0), target)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - t, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > float(target):
            lo = t
        else:
            hi = t
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return fix_sum(x, target)


def interp_to(src_h, src_n, dst_n):
    src_h = np.asarray(src_h, dtype=float)
    if src_h.size != src_n:
        src_h = src_h[:src_n]
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return src_h.copy()
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(src_n)
    xq = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(dst_n)
    return np.interp(xq, xp, src_h)


def make_q(h):
    b = 1.0 - h
    return np.convolve(h, b[::-1], mode="full")


def c5_of_q(q, n):
    return float(np.max(q)) * (2.0 / float(n))


def c5_of_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    q = make_q(h)
    return c5_of_q(q, n)


def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c1 = float(np.max(make_q(h))) * dx
    try:
        c2 = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"))) * dx
        c1 = max(c1, c2)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return c1


def sym_even(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def sym_selfcomp(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    h = 0.5 * (h + 1.0 - h[::-1])
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def make_midpoints(n):
    return (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(n)


def shape_peak(n):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def shape_valley(n):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = np.abs(x - 1.0)
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def shape_v(n, amp):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = 0.5 + amp * (0.5 - np.abs(x - 1.0))
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def shape_ramp_up(n):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = 0.5 * x
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def shape_ramp_down(n):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = 1.0 - 0.5 * x
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def shape_cos(n):
    x = make_midpoints(n)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0))
    return project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)


def add_candidate(lst, h, n):
    if h is None:
        return
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != int(n):
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return
    h = project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)
    c = c5_of_h(h)
    lst.append((c, int(n), h))


def add_lag_gradient(g, h, lag, w):
    n = len(h)
    if abs(w) < 1e-16:
        return
    if lag == 0:
        g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    elif lag > 0:
        m = n - lag
        g[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[lag:lag + m])
        g[lag:] -= w * h[:m]
    else:
        a = -lag
        m = n - a
        g[a:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
        g[:m] -= w * h[a:a + m]


def active_gradient(h, q, beta, max_lags):
    n = len(h)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    tol = 1e-10 + 1e-11 * abs(qmax)
    idx_all = np.flatnonzero(q >= qmax - tol)
    if idx_all.size == 0:
        idx_all = np.arange(len(q))
    if idx_all.size > max_lags:
        if beta <= 0.0:
            idx = idx_all[:max_lags]
            wvals = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / float(max(1, len(idx))))
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(q, -max_lags)[-max_lags:]
            vals = q[idx] - qmax
            exps = np.exp(beta * vals)
            s = float(np.sum(exps))
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                wvals = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / float(max(1, len(idx))))
            else:
                wvals = exps / s
    else:
        idx = idx_all
        if beta <= 0.0:
            wvals = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / float(max(1, len(idx))))
        else:
            vals = q[idx] - qmax
            exps = np.exp(beta * vals)
            s = float(np.sum(exps))
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                wvals = np.full(len(idx), 1.0 / float(max(1, len(idx))))
            else:
                wvals = exps / s
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = n - 1
    for k in range(len(idx)):
        if wvals[k] <= 1e-14:
            continue
        lag = m - int(idx[k])
        add_lag_gradient(g, h, lag, wvals[k])
    return g


def add_coord_update(qout, h, i, delta):
    n = len(h)
    if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
        return
    qout[i:i + n] += delta * (1.0 - h[::-1])
    start = (n - 1) - i
    qout[start:start + n] -= delta * h
    qout[n - 1] -= delta * delta


def pair_move_best(h, q, i, j, inc_first, current_c, fracs):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    if inc_first:
        maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
    else:
        maxd = min(1.0 - h[j], h[i])
    if maxd <= 1e-12:
        return None
    best = None
    for frac in fracs:
        d = frac * maxd
        if d <= 1e-11:
            continue
        h2 = h.copy()
        q2 = q.copy()
        if inc_first:
            add_coord_update(q2, h2, i, d)
            h2[i] = h2[i] + d
            add_coord_update(q2, h2, j, -d)
            h2[j] = h2[j] - d
        else:
            add_coord_update(q2, h2, i, -d)
            h2[i] = h2[i] - d
            add_coord_update(q2, h2, j, d)
            h2[j] = h2[j] + d
        if h2[i] < -1e-11 or h2[i] > 1.0 + 1e-11 or h2[j] < -1e-11 or h2[j] > 1.0 + 1e-11:
            continue
        h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        c = c5_of_q(q2, n)
        if c < current_c - 1e-15:
            if best is None or c < best[2]:
                best = (h2, q2, c)
    return best


def local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, time_alloc, seed):
    global _BUDGET_S, _START_TIME
    start = time.time()
    limit = min(float(time_alloc), max(10.0, remaining_time() - 15.0))
    if limit < 8.0:
        return h, q, best_c
    n = len(h)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_simplex(h, target)
    q = make_q(h)
    cur = c5_of_q(q, n)
    if cur < best_c:
        best_c = cur
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    fracs = (1.0, 0.5, 0.75, 0.25, 0.125, 0.875, 0.375)
    sweeps = 0
    no_imp = 0
    while sweeps < 14 and no_imp < 5 and (time.time() - start) < limit and remaining_time() > 6.0:
        sweeps += 1
        improved = False
        g = active_gradient(h, q, 25.0, 20)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            p = max(4, min(16, n // 4))
            low = np.argsort(g)[:p]
            high = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:p]
            for ii in low:
                for jj in high:
                    if ii == jj:
                        continue
                    mv = pair_move_best(h, q, int(ii), int(jj), True, cur, fracs)
                    if mv is not None:
                        h, q, cur = mv
                        if cur < best_c:
                            best_c = cur
                        improved = True
                    mv = pair_move_best(h, q, int(ii), int(jj), False, cur, fracs)
                    if mv is not None:
                        h, q, cur = mv
                        if cur < best_c:
                            best_c = cur
                        improved = True
                    if (time.time() - start) > limit * 0.75:
                        break
                if (time.time() - start) > limit * 0.75:
                    break
        rnd_count = 450 if n <= 200 else 220
        for _ in range(rnd_count):
            if (time.time() - start) > limit or remaining_time() <= 6.0:
                break
            ii = int(rng.randint(n))
            jj = int(rng.randint(n))
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                mv = pair_move_best(h, q, ii, jj, True, cur, fracs)
            else:
                mv = pair_move_best(h, q, ii, jj, False, cur, fracs)
            if mv is not None:
                h, q, cur = mv
                if cur < best_c:
                    best_c = cur
                improved = True
        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
            if sweeps % 3 == 0:
                h = project_simplex(h, target)
                q = make_q(h)
                cur = c5_of_q(q, n)
                if cur < best_c:
                    best_c = cur
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp == 2 and (time.time() - start) < limit:
                h2 = project_simplex(h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n), target)
                q2 = make_q(h2)
                c2 = c5_of_q(q2, n)
                if c2 < cur + 1e-5:
                    h, q, cur = h2, q2, c2
                    if cur < best_c:
                        best_c = cur
    h = project_simplex(h, target)
    q = make_q(h)
    cur = c5_of_q(q, n)
    if cur < best_c:
        best_c = cur
    return h, q, best_c


def poly_objective(c):
    if _POLY_BASIS is None:
        return 1e9
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(_POLY_N, dtype=float)
    h = h + _POLY_BASIS.dot(c)
    h = project_simplex(h, _POLY_TARGET)
    return c5_of_h(h)


def lowdim_fourier_search(n, ref_h, time_alloc, seed, current_best):
    global _POLY_N, _POLY_BASIS, _POLY_TARGET
    start = time.time()
    limit = min(float(time_alloc), max(10.0, remaining_time() - 20.0))
    if limit < 15.0:
        return None
    if n < 32:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(n) - 1.0
    K = 7
    X = np.empty((n, K), dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        X[:, k - 1] = np.cos(np.pi * k * t)
    _POLY_N = n
    _POLY_BASIS = X
    _POLY_TARGET = n / 2.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_coeff = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    best_obj = poly_objective(best_coeff)
    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=float))
    if ref_h is not None and len(np.asarray(ref_h)) == n:
        try:
            er = project_simplex(np.asarray(ref_h, dtype=float), n / 2.0)
            best_lsq = np.linalg.lstsq(X, er - 0.5, rcond=None)[0]
            best_lsq = np.clip(best_lsq, -0.9, 0.9)
            starts.append(best_lsq)
        except Exception:
            pass
    vcoeff = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    vcoeff[0] = 0.55
    starts.append(vcoeff)
    vcoeff2 = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    vcoeff2[0] = -0.45
    vcoeff2[2] = 0.35
    starts.append(vcoeff2)
    n_random = 260 if limit > 45.0 else 100
    for r in range(n_random):
        if (time.time() - start) > limit or remaining_time() <= 25.0:
            break
        coeff = rng.uniform(-0.62, 0.62, K)
        obj = poly_objective(coeff)
        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
            best_obj = obj
            best_coeff = coeff.copy()
        if r % 40 == 0 and len(starts) < 10:
            starts.append(coeff.copy())
    for cs in starts:
        if (time.time() - start) > limit or remaining_time() <= 30.0:
            break
        cs = np.clip(np.asarray(cs, dtype=float).copy(), -0.9, 0.9)
        try:
            res = minimize(poly_objective, cs, method="Powell",
                           options={"maxiter": 120, "xtol": 1e-8, "gtol": 1e-8})
            coord = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(coord)):
                coord = np.clip(coord, -0.95, 0.95)
                obj = poly_objective(coord)
                if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                    best_obj = obj
                    best_coeff = coord.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj) or best_obj > current_best + 1e-12:
        return None
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    h = h + _POLY_BASIS.dot(np.clip(best_coeff, -0.95, 0.95))
    h = project_simplex(h, n / 2.0)
    c = c5_of_h(h)
    if c < current_best + 1e-12:
        return (h, c, n)
    return None


def smooth_obj(x):
    q = make_q(x)
    mx = float(np.max(q))
    shifted = q - mx
    exps = np.exp(_BETA * shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(exps))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        s = 1.0
    val = mx + math.log(s) / _BETA
    return float(val) * _DX


def smooth_jac(x):
    q = make_q(x)
    mx = float(np.max(q))
    shifted = q - mx
    exps = np.exp(_BETA * shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(exps))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    w = exps / s
    n = len(x)
    m = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for p in range(len(w)):
        ww = w[p] * _DX
        if ww > 1e-12:
            lag = m - p
            add_lag_gradient(g, x, lag, ww)
    return g


def con_sum(x):
    return float(np.sum(x)) - float(_TGT)


def slsqp_polish(h, time_alloc, seed):
    global _N, _TGT, _DX, _BETA
    start = time.time()
    limit = min(float(time_alloc), max(10.0, remaining_time() - 25.0))
    n = len(h)
    _N = n
    _TGT = n / 2.0
    _DX = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = project_simplex(h, _TGT)
    c = c5_of_h(h)
    if limit < 25.0 or n > 230:
        return h, c
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': con_sum}]
    for beta in (35.0, 120.0, 280.0):
        if (time.time() - start) > limit or remaining_time() <= 30.0:
            break
        _BETA = beta
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj, h, method="SLSQP",
                           bounds=bounds,
                           constraints=cons,
                           jac=smooth_jac,
                           options={"maxiter": 60, "ftol": 1e-12, "iprint": 0})
            h2 = project_simplex(res.x, _TGT)
            c2 = c5_of_h(h2)
            if c2 < c - 1e-15:
                h, c = h2, c2
        except Exception:
            pass
    h = project_simplex(h, _TGT)
    c = c5_of_h(h)
    return h, c


def line_search_return(h, best_c, direction, target, dx):
    lrs = (0.25, 0.08, 0.5, 0.12, 0.03, 0.008, 0.002, 0.8, 0.0008)
    for lr in lrs:
        h2 = project_simplex(h + lr * direction, target)
        q2 = make_q(h2)
        c2 = c5_of_q(q2, len(h))
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
            return h2, q2, c2
    return None, None, None


def optimize_full(h, time_alloc, seed):
    global _N, _TGT, _DX
    start = time.time()
    n = len(h)
    _N = n
    _TGT = n / 2.0
    _DX = 2.0 / float(n)
    limit = min(float(time_alloc), max(20.0, remaining_time() - 35.0))
    if limit < 25.0:
        h = project_simplex(h, _TGT)
        return h, c5_of_h(h), n
    h = project_simplex(h, _TGT)
    q = make_q(h)
    cur = c5_of_q(q, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cur
    beta_values = (0.0, 12.0, 55.0, 180.0)
    beta_iters = (90, 60, 40, 30)
    for bi in range(len(beta_values)):
        if (time.time() - start) > limit * 0.55 or remaining_time() <= 60.0:
            break
        beta = beta_values[bi]
        momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for it in range(beta_iters[bi]):
            if (time.time() - start) > limit * 0.65 or remaining_time() <= 60.0:
                break
            g = active_gradient(h, q, beta, 24)
            norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if norm < 1e-13:
                break
            plain_dir = -g / norm
            if it == 0:
                momentum = plain_dir.copy()
            else:
                momentum = 0.65 * momentum + 0.35 * plain_dir
            mn = float(np.linalg.norm(momentum))
            if mn < 1e-13:
                direction = plain_dir
            else:
                direction = momentum / mn
            nh, nq, nc = line_search_return(h, best_c, direction, _TGT, _DX)
            if nh is None:
                nh, nq, nc = line_search_return(h, best_c, plain_dir, _TGT, _DX)
            if nh is None:
                break
            h, q, cur = nh, nq, nc
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
    if (time.time() - start) < limit * 0.85 and remaining_time() > 45.0:
        elapsed = time.time() - start
        pair_alloc = max(20.0, min(0.45 * limit, limit - elapsed - 30.0))
        if pair_alloc > 20.0:
            h, q, best_c = local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, pair_alloc, seed + 1)
            best_h = h.copy()
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    if elapsed < limit - 50.0 and remaining_time() > 70.0 and n <= 230:
        sl_alloc = max(25.0, min(0.30 * limit, limit - elapsed - 40.0))
        if sl_alloc > 25.0:
            h, best_c = slsqp_polish(h, sl_alloc, seed + 2)
            best_h = h.copy()
            q = make_q(h)
            h, q, best_c = local_pair_search(h, q, best_c, max(15.0, 0.12 * limit), seed + 3)
            best_h = h.copy()
    best_h = project_simplex(best_h, _TGT)
    final_c = c5_of_h(best_h)
    if final_c > best_c + 1e-12:
        best_h = h.copy()
        final_c = c5_of_h(best_h)
    return best_h, final_c, n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _START_TIME, _BUDGET_S
    _START_TIME = time.time()
    _BUDGET_S = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent_h = None
    parent_n = 0
    try:
        ph = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if ph.ndim == 1 and ph.size > 0:
            parent_h = ph.copy()
            parent_n = len(ph)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    cand_list = []

    if parent_h is not None:
        add_candidate(cand_list, parent_h, parent_n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, sym_even(parent_h), parent_n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, sym_selfcomp(parent_h), parent_n)
        for s in (0.03, 0.08, 0.15):
            add_candidate(cand_list, parent_h + rng.normal(0.0, s, parent_n), parent_n)
    else:
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_v(160, math.sqrt(9.0 / 13.0)), 160)

    ref_n = parent_n if parent_n > 0 else 160
    n_set = set()
    for nn in (ref_n, ref_n - 8, ref_n + 8, 96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 320):
        if 64 <= nn <= 384:
            n_set.add(int(nn))

    n_pairs = []
    for nn in n_set:
        n_pairs.append((abs(int(nn) - int(ref_n)), int(nn)))
    n_pairs.sort()
    n_ordered = [p[1] for p in n_pairs]
    if len(n_ordered) > 12:
        n_ordered = n_ordered[:12]

    vamp = math.sqrt(9.0 / 13.0)
    for n in n_ordered:
        target = n / 2.0
        if parent_h is not None:
            add_candidate(cand_list, interp_to(parent_h, parent_n, n), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_peak(n), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_valley(n), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_v(n, vamp), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_v(n, 0.78), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_v(n, 0.88), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_ramp_up(n), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_ramp_down(n), n)
        add_candidate(cand_list, shape_cos(n), n)
        for _ in range(3):
            add_candidate(cand_list, rng.rand(n), n)
        for _ in range(2):
            hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            idx = rng.permutation(n)
            hb[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
            if n % 2 == 1:
                hb[idx[n // 2]] = 0.5
            add_candidate(cand_list, hb, n)

    if len(cand_list) == 0:
        n = 160
        h = shape_v(n, vamp)
        cand_list.append((c5_of_h(h), n, h))

    order = []
    for idx, cand in enumerate(cand_list):
        order.append((cand[0], idx, cand[1]))
    order.sort()
    sorted_cands = []
    for cnt, idx, nn in order:
        sorted_cands.append(cand_list[idx])

    best_c = sorted_cands[0][0]
    best_n = sorted_cands[0][1]
    best_h = project_simplex(sorted_cands[0][2], best_n / 2.0)
    best_c = c5_of_h(best_h)

    opt_starts = [(best_h, best_c, best_n)]
    seen_ns = set()
    seen_ns.add(best_n)
    for cnt, nn, hh in sorted_cands:
        if len(opt_starts) >= 5:
            break
        if cnt > best_c + 0.003:
            break
        if int(nn) in seen_ns:
            continue
        seen_ns.add(int(nn))
        h2 = project_simplex(hh, nn / 2.0)
        c2 = c5_of_h(h2)
        opt_starts.append((h2, c2, int(nn)))
    if remaining_time() > 520.0:
        pert = project_simplex(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, best_n), best_n / 2.0)
        opt_starts.append((pert, c5_of_h(pert), best_n))

    if remaining_time() > 220.0:
        low_alloc = min(80.0, max(25.0, remaining_time() - 180.0))
        res = lowdim_fourier_search(best_n, best_h, low_alloc, seed + 101, best_c)
        if res is not None:
            h2, c2, n2 = res
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2
                opt_starts[0] = (best_h, best_c, best_n)
        if remaining_time() > 260.0:
            alt_n = best_n + 32
            if alt_n > 384:
                alt_n = max(96, best_n - 24)
            res = lowdim_fourier_search(alt_n, None, min(50.0, remaining_time() - 200.0), seed + 102, best_c)
            if res is not None:
                h2, c2, n2 = res
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2
                    if len(opt_starts) > 0:
                        opt_starts[0] = (best_h, best_c, best_n)
                    else:
                        opt_starts.append((best_h, best_c, best_n))

    for si in range(len(opt_starts)):
        if remaining_time() < 130.0:
            break
        h0, c0, n0 = opt_starts[si]
        if si == 0:
            alloc = min(560.0, max(120.0, remaining_time() - 180.0))
        else:
            alloc = min(260.0, max(80.0, remaining_time() - 180.0))
        if alloc < 80.0:
            continue
        h1, c1, n1 = optimize_full(h0, alloc, seed + 1000 + si * 97)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, n1
            if si == 0:
                opt_starts[0] = (best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if remaining_time() > 90.0:
        h0 = project_simplex(best_h, best_n / 2.0)
        c0 = c5_of_h(h0)
        if c0 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h0, c0
        alloc = min(180.0, max(35.0, remaining_time() - 35.0))
        q0 = make_q(best_h)
        h1, q1, c1 = local_pair_search(best_h, q0, best_c, alloc, seed + 505)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if remaining_time() > 70.0 and best_n <= 230:
        h1, c1 = slsqp_polish(best_h, min(55.0, max(25.0, remaining_time() - 35.0)), seed + 777)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if remaining_time() > 35.0:
        q0 = make_q(best_h)
        h1, q1, c1 = local_pair_search(best_h, q0, best_c, min(25.0, remaining_time() - 10.0), seed + 909)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    best_h = np.clip(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = best_n / 2.0
    s0 = float(np.sum(best_h))
    if abs(s0 - target) > 1e-10:
        best_h = fix_sum(best_h, target)
        best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)

    final_c = c5_exact(best_h)
    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = best_c
    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        ec = float(ec)
        if np.isfinite(ec) and ec < final_c + 1e-12:
            final_c = ec
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n))
```
